                                var width = $locationHelper.outerWidth();
                                var margin = -(width / 2 - 10);

                                            $locationHelper.remove();
                                        }, 315);

<script>
/*<![CDATA[*/

            window.arrOnReady = window.arrOnReady || [];
            window.arrOnReady.push(function () {
                var $modalLocation = $('.modal-location');
                var $row = $modalLocation.find('.modal-location-row');
                var $parts = $row.children('.modal-location-part');
                var $part1 = $parts.eq(0);
                var $part2 = $parts.eq(1);
                var $part3 = $parts.eq(2);
                var $part4 = $parts.eq(3);

                var windowWidth;

                var checkPosition = function () {
                    windowWidth = $(window).width();

                    $parts.detach();

                    if (windowWidth > 991) {
                        $row.append($part1);
                        $row.append($part2);
                        $row.append($part3);
                        $row.append($part4);
                    } else {
                        $row.append($part1);
                        $row.append($part3);
                        $row.append($part2);
                        $row.append($part4);
                    }
                };

                checkPosition();

                $(window).on('resize', checkPosition);
            });
        
/*]]>*/
</script>
